# SETUP symbol map: 1805 functions of its own + 185 library (LIB) + 2819 data objects, base 0x00566B00
# Seeded by `eqmodule.py seed`, then kept BY HAND: rename a func_ entry to
# the mangled name your object defines when you write that function.
# LIB is libgcc/soft-float/crt (GPL runtime), not decompiled.
# A trailing `# ...` is a HINT, never a name (eqmodule.hints): `File.cpp:N`
# the function asserts from there (a fact), `File.cpp~` it sits between two
# that do (inferred), `old:<label>` the old claude-eqoa-iso map's guess.
# 0 assert-anchored, 0 by span, 488 old labels (4 moved up to the real start, 228 jump-table labels dropped).
# 2026-10-16: 911 entries named from the 2002 betas' OWN export tables (decomp-builds/names/SETUP.candidates.txt,
# copies=1 only: body identical with relocations masked and unique in both builds). Marked `beta-export`;
# measured on SUPPORT's real names, about 1% of such names are a renamed or sibling function.  Trust a name once your object matches.
